<span class="ct-node-label">Affiliate Pages</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Making money from websites containing affiliate links is possible, but it is not as simple as publishing product round-ups and waiting for commissions. The basic model is straightforward: a website recommends a product or service, the reader clicks a tracked link, and the site owner earns a commission if that click leads to a qualifying purchase or action.</span>
</span>
